- original:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Hiawatha, leader of The Iroquois (Player 4).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: The Iroquois
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Mohawk Warrior, Replacing Swordsman (Requires technology: Iron Working)
        - Unique Unit: Prowler, Replacing Musketman (Requires technology: Metallurgy)
        - Unique Unit: Tadodaho, Replacing Great Diplomat (Requires technology: None)
        - Unique Building: Longhouse, Replacing Herbalist (Requires technology: Calendar)
        - Civilization Ability: Units move through Forest and Jungle as if they were roads, and these tiles establish City Connections. Military Land Units start with the Woodsman Promotion. Gain 15% progress towards the next Tadodaho in your Capital when adopting a Social Policy.
      - Leader: Hiawatha
      - PlayerID: 4
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late game Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      - Lasers: Allows you to build the Nuclear Missile, a frightening weapon that requires Uranium, and is capable of destroying units and cities.
        Completing it would unlock: Particle Physics, Globalization, Nuclear Fusion
      - The Internet: Increases Tourism output of all cities by 50%, helping you advance toward a Cultural Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Nanotechnology, Particle Physics, Globalization
      
      ## Policies
      - Martial Spirit (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Martial Spirit: +25% Combat Strength when attacking in melee for 50 turns, scaling with game speed. War Weariness reduced by 25%, and Razing Speed is doubled.
      - Total War (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Total War: +25% Production when building Land Units. Warscore increases 25% more quickly, and it is 25% easier to bully City-States.
      - Military-Industrial Complex (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Military-Industrial Complex: -33% Gold cost of purchasing units. +3 Science from Defense Buildings, Citadels, Forts, and Unique Improvements.
      - Elite Forces (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Elite Forces: Newly created Military Units receive +15 Experience. Military Units gain 50% more Experience from combat.
      - Third Alternative (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Third Alternative: Quantity of Strategic Resources produced (including Paper) is increased by 100%. Reduces Unit Gold Maintenance costs by 25%.
      - Syndicalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Syndicalism: Corporate Franchises count double in the Cities of Civilizations you are at least Popular with, and this extra Franchise does not count against your Global Franchise maximum. Can establish 5 additional Franchises in foreign Cities. +2 Production from all Corporation Offices.
      - New World Order (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): New World Order: -2 Unhappiness from and Distress in all Cities. Police Stations and Constabularies provide +3 Culture and +5 Production, and are constructed 100% more quickly.
      - Lightning Warfare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lightning Warfare: +3 Movement for Great Generals. Gunpowder units gain +15% attack and ignore enemy ZOC; Armored units gain +15% attack and +1 Movement.
      - Police State (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Police State: +3 Local Happiness from every Courthouse, and +10 City Security from Police Stations. +100% Production towards Courthouses and Police Stations.
      - Ceremony (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * +25% Production toward National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * Royal Astrologer built in Capital (+3 Science, +1 Science to all Councils, Smokehouses, and Herbalists; 1 Scientist Slot).
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - Mare Nostrum (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Mare Nostrum: +1 Oil and Coal for every City-State Alliance. +2 Production and Culture to Atolls, Fishing Boats, and Offshore Oil Platforms. Naval Ranged Units gain the Mare Nostrum Promotion.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Regimental System (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Earn Great Generals and Great Admirals 33% faster.
        - * Great Generals and Great Admirals gain the Regimental Traditions Promotion (+10% Leadership Combat Bonus and +1 Tile Radius).
        - * +2 additional copies of the unique Luxury Resources from expending Great Admirals for their Voyage of Discovery ability.
        - * Military Units can be upgraded in territory owned by Vassals and friendly City-States.
      - Entrepreneurship (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from Workshops.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from every Mine, Quarry, and Lumber Mill.
        - * +25% Yields when you expend Great Merchants or Great Engineers for their Instant Yield abilities.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Trade Confederacy (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +25% Yields for International Trade Routes.
        - * Trade Routes to City-States generate +1 Influence per turn (with the target City-State) per each owned City-State Trade Route (up to +5).
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Foreign Service (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 100 Spy Points.
        - * Earn Great Diplomats 50% faster.
        - * +50% Rewards from City-State Quests.
        - * +1 of every Strategic Resource for every three City-State Alliances you maintain.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 8
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 1
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 3
        - Boldness: 2
        - WarBias: 3
        - HostileBias: 8
        - WarmongerHate: 5
        - NeutralBias: 5
        - FriendlyBias: 5
        - GuardedBias: 6
        - AfraidBias: 9
        - DiplomaticBalance: 4
        - Friendliness: 5
        - WorkWithWillingness: 7
        - WorkAgainstWillingness: 9
        - Loyalty: 10
        - MinorCivFriendlyBias: 7
        - MinorCivNeutralBias: 4
        - MinorCivHostileBias: 6
        - MinorCivWarBias: 1
        - DenounceWillingness: 2
        - Forgiveness: 4
        - Meanness: 3
        - Neediness: 2
        - Chattiness: 6
        - DeceptiveBias: 3
        - Rationale: Slight persona adjustments for strategic flexibility. WarBias increased from 2 to 3 (still low) - recognizing need for contingency planning if defensive posture requires limited counteroffensives. GuardedBias raised to 6 (was 5) - cautious approach given overwhelming threats remains appropriate. Forgiveness reduced to 4 (was 6) - less lenient toward aggressive actions from Poland/Persia given existential military disparity. WorkAgainstWillingness at 9 to leverage any opportunities to coordinate against India's diplomatic victory bid. These are modest refinements supporting the defensive Spaceship strategy.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Mobile Tactics
        - Rationale: Researching Mobile Tactics to unlock Telecommunications (first nuclear technology) and Stealth. With Poland's overwhelming military advantage (20k vs 4k) and active war, nuclear deterrence capability is essential. Stealth technology also improves defensive reconnaissance. This path aligns with science victory while providing defensive options against existential military threats.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Lightning Warfare (Policy)
        - Rationale: Adopting Lightning Warfare from Autocracy (Level 2 Tenet) improves military effectiveness: +3 Movement for Great Generals, gunpowder units gain +15% attack and ignore enemy ZOC, armored units gain +15% attack and +1 Movement. These bonuses strengthen defensive capabilities against Poland's superior forces, improving ability to withdraw and counterattack strategically without shifting resources away from science victory infrastructure.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### The Celts
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: The Celts are effectively eliminated with only Dublin remaining at 74% health and my overwhelming military advantage (855 vs 75). Resetting relationship modifiers to neutral as they will soon be defeated. This prevents excessive diplomatic penalties for warmongering and allows AI to prepare for post-war diplomatic environment.
        - UpdatedTurn: 184
        
        ### Denmark
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Denmark effectively eliminated with only Copenhagen remaining at -20% health and my overwhelming military advantage near their capital. Resetting to neutral stance as the war will conclude imminently. This removes wartime modifiers that are no longer needed and prepares for post-war diplomatic environment, preventing excessive warmonger penalties while focusing attention on remaining threats (Poland, Persia) and India's diplomatic victory threat.
        - UpdatedTurn: 270
        
        ### The Maya
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Resetting Maya relationship to neutral following their capitulation as India's vassal in turn 344. The previous positive modifiers (Public: 25, Private: 20) from turn 334 are no longer appropriate since Maya is no longer an independent voting partner against India. Their votes now serve India's interests, making the previous alliance value diminished. Maintaining a neutral stance prevents excessive hostility while acknowledging the changed diplomatic reality where we must compete with India directly rather than through Maya as a proxy.
        - UpdatedTurn: 346
        
        ### Persia
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Confirming neutral stance with Persia. While they destroyed my Dreadnought (hostile act), previous relationship modifiers were already reset. Persia is secondary threat compared to Poland's overwhelming military (19,902 vs 7,524). Focus diplomatic AI decision-making on Poland and India threats without unnecessary modifiers on Persia.
        - UpdatedTurn: 388
        
        ### Poland
        - Public: -20
        - Private: -30
        - Rationale: Increasing Poland relationship from -40/-50 to -20/-30. Poland signed peace treaty with city-states (Persia/Raqmu) which may indicate reduced aggression. Though still overwhelming military threat (20,343 vs 4,023), slight moderation prevents AI from overcommitting resources when main threat is Poland focus. Defensive posture (Defense 95) unchanged - this affects diplomatic calculations, not combat priorities. Allows strategic flexibility while maintaining appropriate caution toward stronger enemy.
        - UpdatedTurn: 396
        
        ### India
        - Public: -20
        - Private: -20
        - Rationale: Maintaining India relationship at -20/-20. India is diplomatic victory threat (90% progress, voting in 4 turns), but our military (4,023) is 7.5x weaker. Moderate hostility acknowledges threat while avoiding complete antagonism. Open Borders with India provides science bonuses (+27 from trade routes). Can't afford full hostility when focused on science victory. Targeting India's World Religion: Hinduism repeal提案, but diplomatic flexibility needed if repeal fails.
        - UpdatedTurn: 396
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: Maintaining current strategic priorities. Apollo Program construction continues in Osininka (4 turns, 494 PPT) - critical for science victory. Defense remains highest priority (95) given Poland's overwhelming 5x military advantage and active warfare. Production focus on spaceship parts after Apollo completion. Nuke flavor at 90 for deterrence if uranium secured. Gold at 85 managing -22 GPT economic strain. Science (100) and Spaceship (100) flavors drive victory path while defensive posture protects against Poland and Persia threats.
        - GrandStrategy: Spaceship

          - Sanction: Persia:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: 382
          - Open Door: Veligrad:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: 382
          - World Religion: Hinduism:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: 382
          - Passport System:
            - Description: Increases international travel between Civilizations. Increases the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 50%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - EnactedOn: 382
          - Indian Sphere of Influence: Sofia:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 382
          - Scholars In Residence:
            - Description: Civilizations have -20% Research cost for Technologies already discovered by another Civilization. Additional -2% for every City-State Ally, up to -50%.
            - EnactedOn: 382
          - Indian Sphere of Influence: Vatican City:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 388
          - Open Door: Pago Pago:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: 382
          - Global Peace Accords:
            - Description: Unit maintenance costs are raised by 25% Gold. 2.0x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 0.5x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: 382
          - Open Door: Okilis:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: 382
        - Proposals:
          - Repeal: World Religion: Hinduism:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - Proposer: The Iroquois
          - Enact: Polish Sphere of Influence: Colombo:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - Proposer: Poland
          - Enact: World Ideology: Order:
            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology. Civilizations following the Ideology get an additional Delegate, plus 1 Delegate for every other Civilization that follows the Ideology. Public opinion in favor of that Ideology is increased for all Civilizations. Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission.
            - Proposer: India

        You, Hiawatha (leader of The Iroquois, Player 4), are making strategic decisions after turn 398.
- replay:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Hiawatha, leader of The Iroquois (Player 4).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: The Iroquois
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Mohawk Warrior, Replacing Swordsman (Requires technology: Iron Working)
        - Unique Unit: Prowler, Replacing Musketman (Requires technology: Metallurgy)
        - Unique Unit: Tadodaho, Replacing Great Diplomat (Requires technology: None)
        - Unique Building: Longhouse, Replacing Herbalist (Requires technology: Calendar)
        - Civilization Ability: Units move through Forest and Jungle as if they were roads, and these tiles establish City Connections. Military Land Units start with the Woodsman Promotion. Gain 15% progress towards the next Tadodaho in your Capital when adopting a Social Policy.
      - Leader: Hiawatha
      - PlayerID: 4
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late game Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      - Lasers: Allows you to build the Nuclear Missile, a frightening weapon that requires Uranium, and is capable of destroying units and cities.
        Completing it would unlock: Particle Physics, Globalization, Nuclear Fusion
      - The Internet: Increases Tourism output of all cities by 50%, helping you advance toward a Cultural Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Nanotechnology, Particle Physics, Globalization
      
      ## Policies
      - Martial Spirit (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Martial Spirit: +25% Combat Strength when attacking in melee for 50 turns, scaling with game speed. War Weariness reduced by 25%, and Razing Speed is doubled.
      - Total War (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Total War: +25% Production when building Land Units. Warscore increases 25% more quickly, and it is 25% easier to bully City-States.
      - Military-Industrial Complex (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Military-Industrial Complex: -33% Gold cost of purchasing units. +3 Science from Defense Buildings, Citadels, Forts, and Unique Improvements.
      - Elite Forces (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Elite Forces: Newly created Military Units receive +15 Experience. Military Units gain 50% more Experience from combat.
      - Third Alternative (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Third Alternative: Quantity of Strategic Resources produced (including Paper) is increased by 100%. Reduces Unit Gold Maintenance costs by 25%.
      - Syndicalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Syndicalism: Corporate Franchises count double in the Cities of Civilizations you are at least Popular with, and this extra Franchise does not count against your Global Franchise maximum. Can establish 5 additional Franchises in foreign Cities. +2 Production from all Corporation Offices.
      - New World Order (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): New World Order: -2 Unhappiness from and Distress in all Cities. Police Stations and Constabularies provide +3 Culture and +5 Production, and are constructed 100% more quickly.
      - Lightning Warfare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lightning Warfare: +3 Movement for Great Generals. Gunpowder units gain +15% attack and ignore enemy ZOC; Armored units gain +15% attack and +1 Movement.
      - Police State (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Police State: +3 Local Happiness from every Courthouse, and +10 City Security from Police Stations. +100% Production towards Courthouses and Police Stations.
      - Ceremony (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * +25% Production toward National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * Royal Astrologer built in Capital (+3 Science, +1 Science to all Councils, Smokehouses, and Herbalists; 1 Scientist Slot).
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - Mare Nostrum (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Mare Nostrum: +1 Oil and Coal for every City-State Alliance. +2 Production and Culture to Atolls, Fishing Boats, and Offshore Oil Platforms. Naval Ranged Units gain the Mare Nostrum Promotion.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Regimental System (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Earn Great Generals and Great Admirals 33% faster.
        - * Great Generals and Great Admirals gain the Regimental Traditions Promotion (+10% Leadership Combat Bonus and +1 Tile Radius).
        - * +2 additional copies of the unique Luxury Resources from expending Great Admirals for their Voyage of Discovery ability.
        - * Military Units can be upgraded in territory owned by Vassals and friendly City-States.
      - Entrepreneurship (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from Workshops.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from every Mine, Quarry, and Lumber Mill.
        - * +25% Yields when you expend Great Merchants or Great Engineers for their Instant Yield abilities.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Trade Confederacy (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +25% Yields for International Trade Routes.
        - * Trade Routes to City-States generate +1 Influence per turn (with the target City-State) per each owned City-State Trade Route (up to +5).
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Foreign Service (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 100 Spy Points.
        - * Earn Great Diplomats 50% faster.
        - * +50% Rewards from City-State Quests.
        - * +1 of every Strategic Resource for every three City-State Alliances you maintain.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Rationale: Selecting New World Order (Autocracy Level 1 Tenet) to address our empire's unhappiness (-2 per city, which is 30 global happiness) and remove the combat penalty. This policy also provides +5 Production and +3 Culture to every Constabulary and Police Station, which we have in almost all cities, greatly accelerating our spaceship part production and future policy adoption.
        - Policy: New World Order
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: Setting highly hostile relationship modifiers with India to ensure our AI prioritizes voting to repeal World Religion: Hinduism and opposing their World Ideology: Order proposal in the upcoming World Congress vote in 1 turn. India is at 41/43 delegates needed for diplomatic victory, and we must oppose them aggressively.
        - Private: -50
        - TargetID: 6
        - Public: -50
    - #2:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - TargetID: 1
        - Private: 30
        - Rationale: Setting positive relationship modifiers to encourage our AI to seek and accept peace with Persia. We are currently winning or holding our own against them (war score +4%), and securing peace on our southern/eastern borders will allow us to consolidate our defense against Poland and focus on our science victory.
        - Public: 30
    - #3:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: Setting positive relationship modifiers to encourage our AI to seek peace with Poland if an opportunity arises. Poland's military strength is overwhelmingly superior (20k vs 3.5k), and while we are holding our defensive lines successfully, a peace treaty would allow us to safely build our spaceship parts.
        - Private: 20
        - TargetID: 3
        - Public: 20
    - #4:
      - toolName: set-persona
      - args:
        - AfraidBias: 9
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 10
        - WorkAgainstWillingness: 10
        - Boldness: 3
        - GuardedBias: 7
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 8
        - Rationale: Maximizing VictoryCompetitiveness (10) and MinorCivCompetitiveness (8) to force our AI to aggressively compete for city-state alliances, which is India's primary source of delegates. Breaking India's alliances is critical to prevent their imminent diplomatic victory. GuardedBias (7) and AfraidBias (9) remain high to reflect our realistic caution toward Poland's superior military force.
        - HostileBias: 8
    - #5:
      - toolName: set-flavors
      - args:
        - GrandStrategy: Spaceship
        - Flavors:
          - Defense: 95
          - Diplomacy: 40
          - Air: 90
          - Espionage: 80
          - Production: 100
          - Religion: 10
          - Wonder: 10
          - Gold: 90
          - CityDefense: 90
          - Antiair: 90
          - Happiness: 90
          - Ranged: 90
          - Science: 100
          - Spaceship: 100
      - rationale: Maintaining our Spaceship grand strategy for a science victory while fine-tuning flavors. Gold is increased to 90 to address the negative budget (-35 GPT), and Happiness is increased to 90 to prioritize happiness buildings alongside the New World Order policy to eliminate the combat penalty. Strong defensive, ranged, and air flavors are maintained to protect our territory against Poland and Persia.

  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Assessing Current Game State**
            
            I'm currently analyzing our position, turn by turn. My focus is on evaluating incoming threats and the viability of our current victory strategies. I'm also starting to consider the immediate tactical choices ahead.
            
            **Evaluating Victory Conditions**
            
            I'm reviewing our current standing, specifically our score and victory progress. India and Poland are ahead in science, but I'm assessing if a domination push is still feasible given our city count and their positions. I'm also checking the construction progress of the Apollo program.
            
            **Prioritizing Immediate Threats**
            
            I'm shifting my focus to the imminent Diplomatic victory threat from India; they're only two delegates away with voting commencing next turn. Science is close, but that diplomatic win requires immediate attention. I'm also noting the slight lag on Apollo, but the core science output remains strong.
            
            **Disrupting India's Victory**
            
            I've identified a critical opportunity: repealing Hinduism. This action would significantly reduce India's diplomatic delegates. I'm also weighing the implications of India's proposed "World Ideology: Order," which would further bolster their delegate count. My immediate priority is to prevent both diplomatic and cultural victories.
            
            **Researching Next Technologies**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our research path. While Mobile Tactics and Lasers are appealing, the Internet's tourism boost and further unlocks are increasingly attractive, especially given India's cultural progress. My immediate concern is how to acquire Uranium, as it impacts future options like Nuclear Missiles, if we decide to pursue that line.
            
            **Revising Research Focus**
            
            I'm reassessing our technology path. While "Advanced Ballistics" is currently in progress, I'm debating switching our *next* research target from "Mobile Tactics" to either "Lasers" for advanced military units or "The Internet" for a significant tourism boost. The ongoing wars with Poland and Persia are influencing this decision, and I'm ensuring our research aligns with our immediate military needs and overall victory strategy.
            
            **Addressing Overwhelming Odds**
            
            I'm currently grappling with the immense military disparity against Poland and Persia. Despite this, our strong defensive structures are proving effective. I'm prioritizing unit and defense production, particularly the impending completion of the Heavy Bomber and Apollo Program, while reassessing Akwesasme's current production focus.
            
            **Analyzing Defensive Strength**
            
            I'm reviewing our recent city construction, noting Buffalo Creek's Strategic Defense System. I'm now cross-referencing this with troop deployments, particularly the significant Polish forces near Aarhus and Seoul, and assessing the efficacy of our current defensive lineup against their advancements, including the rather alarming Giant Death Robot in Seoul.
            
            **Defending Strategic Locations**
            
            I'm intensely focused on reinforcing our coastal defenses, particularly around Seoul and near Copenhagen, where Polish naval strength is significant. Despite losing a Machine Gun to Persia, our combined arms approach successfully destroyed their Dreadnought. I'm prioritizing science and production to counter these overwhelming odds.
            
            **Reviewing Policy Options**
            
            I'm re-examining our active policies and the next available choices. It seems "Lightning Warfare" was the last adopted Autocracy tenet, and I'm now evaluating if that's still the optimal path forward or if other branches like "Fealty" or "Authority" offer better strategic advantages given the current geopolitical landscape.
            
            **Adopting New Autocracy Policies**
            
            I'm reconsidering our next Autocracy policy. While "Lightning Warfare" looks strong for active combat, I've discovered "Third Alternative." It drastically reduces unit maintenance costs, which is crucial given our current negative GPT, and doubles strategic resources. This seems significantly more beneficial than previously thought.
            
            **Adopting Beneficial Policies**
            
            I'm now seriously considering the "New World Order" Autocracy policy. The happiness boost it provides would be incredibly valuable, especially with our current unhappiness penalties impacting combat effectiveness. This seems more pressing than pure military bonuses or maintenance reduction.
            
            **Adopting Happiness Policies**
            
            I'm now prioritizing policies that will alleviate our significant unhappiness. "New World Order" offers a massive global reduction of 30 unhappiness, which outweighs the localized happiness from "Police State." I'm leaning towards this to bolster our overall stability and combat effectiveness.
            
            **Selecting Optimal Autocracy Tenets**
            
            I'm now evaluating the hierarchy of Autocracy tenets. "New World Order," a Level 1 tenet offering significant happiness, seems the most immediate priority to counter our unhappiness penalty and combat penalties. I'm also considering the synergy of "Military-Industrial Complex" with our defensive structures, and how it fits within our available tenet slots, prioritizing happiness over combat or gold cost reduction for now.
            
            **Selecting Next Autocracy Tenet**
            
            I'm revisiting our Autocracy choices. With "United Front" and "Futurism" already selected, I can choose a Level 2 tenet. However, I'm re-evaluating the option of adopting another Level 1 tenet, specifically "New World Order," for its substantial unhappiness reduction. I'm comparing this benefit to "Total War" and "Lightning Warfare" for their military advantages, but the happiness boost seems paramount given our current penalties.
            
            **Prioritizing Resource Management**
            
            I'm reassessing our current bottlenecks. While production and science are strong, the impact of negative GPT on unit maintenance and potential science penalties is significant. I'm exploring how policies like "Third Alternative" can alleviate these issues, enabling us to focus on critical projects like the Apollo Program and spaceship parts, especially considering our ample gold reserves and available strategic resources.
            
            **Selecting Optimal Autocracy Tenets**
            
            I'm prioritizing "New World Order" for its substantial happiness and production bonuses from our existing Police Stations and Constabularies. This seems more impactful than other options for our current situation.
            
            **Adopting Potent Policies**
            
            I'm now convinced that "New World Order" is the optimal Autocracy choice. Its significant happiness and production boosts from existing police and constabulary buildings directly address our core issues. This policy offers far greater strategic advantage than military-focused options like "Lightning Warfare" given our current diplomatic and cultural victory threats.
            
            **Selecting Optimal Autocracy Tenets**
            
            I'm confirming "New World Order" as the best next step. The global happiness boost it provides is critical to removing combat penalties and significantly enhances production, which will accelerate spaceship construction. This outweighs other immediate options.
            
            **Shifting Strategic Priorities**
            
            My immediate focus is on preventing India's Diplomatic Victory, as voting occurs next turn and they are only two delegates short. I'm also evaluating how to counter their potential World Ideology passage and their cultural advancements, which requires a delicate balance of diplomatic maneuvering and strengthening our own position.
            
            **Influencing World Congress Votes**
            
            I'm strategizing how to use my votes in the upcoming World Congress to thwart India's imminent Diplomatic Victory. My plan is to vote YES on repealing Hinduism, NO on India's World Ideology proposal, and I'm assessing the impact of voting against Poland's Sphere of Influence. I'm now looking into how to best influence the AI's voting decisions to ensure our objectives are met.
            
            **Manipulating International Relations**
            
            I'm currently assessing the impact of further deteriorating relations with India. While increasing hostility would solidify our AI's voting patterns against their diplomatic and religious proposals, I need to consider the significant loss of gold, science, and culture if they were to cancel our numerous active trade routes with them.
            
            **Thwarting India's Victory**
            
            I'm now hyper-focused on preventing India's imminent Diplomatic Victory. They're alarmingly close, just two delegates shy of victory with voting happening next turn. My immediate priority is to disrupt their delegate count, considering the potential consequences for our trade routes if relations sour too much.
            
            **Disrupting India's Delegates**
            
            I'm focusing on how to revoke India's numerous city-state alliances. India boasts 8 allied city-states, granting them substantial delegates. My primary objective is to repeal Hinduism, which also grants India delegates. If Poland, Persia, and I vote for repeal, it will pass.
            
            **Influencing Congress Outcomes**
            
            I'm reassessing the World Congress voting dynamics. India and Maya possess sufficient votes to block the repeal of Hinduism if they prioritize it. I'm devising a strategy to encourage AI allies to vote YES on repealing Hinduism, and critically, to vote NO on India's "World Ideology: Order," aiming to split their delegate focus and prevent their diplomatic victory.
            
            **Seeking Peace Treaties**
            
            I'm re-evaluating the ongoing wars with Poland and Persia. My current focus is on identifying diplomatic pathways to peace. Specifically, I'm exploring how to increase our relationship modifiers to encourage AI allies to accept peace treaties, which would allow us to conserve units and focus on our science victory.
            
            **Securing Peace With Neighbors**
            
            I'm working to negotiate peace treaties, prioritizing Persia to secure my borders and focus on Poland. I've adjusted relationship modifiers to encourage AI allies towards this goal, aiming to conserve forces and re-align efforts. I'm now reassessing Poland's willingness to negotiate given their military strength.
            
            **Targeting India's Diplomacy**
            
            I'm now focusing on isolating India diplomatically. By setting relationships to Public: -50 and Private: -50, I aim to ensure our AI allies perceive them as a major threat, influencing World Congress votes against India. This is critical as they are our primary competitor for the science victory.
            
            **Adjusting Economic Strategy**
            
            I'm reassessing our economic approach; our gold GPT has dipped to -35. I'm considering boosting the Gold flavor to 90 to mitigate this, while maintaining our optimized science and spaceship production flavors. This adjustment is key to sustaining our overall strategy.
            
            **Adjusting Strategy and Flavors**
            
            I'm now adjusting our strategic flavors to prioritize gold and happiness, aiming to counter our negative GPT and unhappiness penalties. Defense, production, and science remain at high levels, with espionage also boosted. My focus is on optimizing our economic stability and overall progress towards victory.
            
            **Boosting Diplomatic Influence**
            
            I'm increasing VictoryCompetitiveness to 10 and MinorCivCompetitiveness to 8 to aggressively counter other civilizations, especially India's city-state alliances, which are key to their diplomatic victory. This should make us more competitive in securing city-states and disrupting their delegate count.
            
            **Refining AI Personalities**
            
            I'm fine-tuning our AI's personality parameters to aggressively counter India's diplomatic victory, increasing competitiveness for victories and city-states. I'm also adjusting biases to reflect caution against Poland's military might while fostering cooperation against common threats, aiming for a more coordinated defense.
            
            **Confirming Research Direction**
            
            I'm confirming that "Mobile Tactics" remains our current research target. While "Lasers" offers advanced weaponry and late-game tech pathways, the immediate benefit of Stealth for defense and its position in our existing research tree makes it the soundest choice. I'm ensuring the system reflects this prior decision.
            
            **Implementing Strategic Changes**
            
            I am now translating our strategic analysis into concrete actions. This involves implementing the "New World Order" policy to combat unhappiness and boost production. Concurrently, I am adjusting diplomatic relations with India, Persia, and Poland to facilitate peace and isolate our primary competitor.
            
            **Applying Policy and Diplomacy**
            
            I'm enacting our chosen Autocracy tenet, "New World Order," to address our critical unhappiness penalties and enhance production. I'm also actively engaging in diplomacy, setting relationships with India to hostile (-50 public/private) to counter their victory, while improving ties with Persia and Poland (30/20 public/private respectively) to secure peace.
            
            **Targeting India's Victory Path**
            
            I'm now focused on dismantling India's diplomatic advantage by severing city-state alliances and targeting their religious delegates. I've established hostile diplomatic stances towards India and amicable ones with Persia and Poland to secure peace and consolidate our efforts against our primary science competitor.
            
            **Executing Diplomatic Maneuvers**
            
            I am enacting immediate diplomatic shifts. India will be set to a hostile relationship (-50 public, -50 private) to disrupt their delegate count. Meanwhile, Persia and Poland will see improved relationships (30 public, 20 public respectively) to foster peace, allowing us to concentrate on our science victory.
            
            **Enacting Policy and Relations**
